
============================================================
=== FINAL GTIR HANDED TO GTFN BACKEND ===
============================================================
apply_divergence_damping_and_update_vn(
  horizontal_gradient_of_normal_wind_divergence,
  next_vn,
  current_vn,
  dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels,
  predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process,
  normal_wind_iau_increment,
  second_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
  theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels,
  horizontal_pressure_gradient,
  horizontal_mask_for_3d_divdamp,
  scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,
  inv_dual_edge_length,
  nudgecoeff_e,
  geofac_grdiv,
  interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
  advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
  advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
  dtime,
  iau_wgt_dyn,
  is_iau_active,
  limited_area,
  apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping,
  apply_4th_order_divergence_damping,
  divdamp_order,
  mean_cell_area,
  second_order_divdamp_factor,
  max_nudging_coefficient,
  dbl_eps,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  _calculate_fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff = λ(
    interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
    divdamp_order,
    mean_cell_area,
    second_order_divdamp_factor
  ) → 
    (λ(interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factorᐞ0) → 
       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
         (⇑(λ(__arg0) → neg(·__arg0)))(interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factorᐞ0), power(mean_cell_area, 2)
       ))(if   divdamp_order == 24
          then (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → maximum(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(
                 0.0, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                        interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor, 0.25 × second_order_divdamp_factor
                      )
               )
          else interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor);
  _calculate_reduced_fourth_order_divdamp_coeff_at_nest_boundary = λ(
    fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
    max_nudging_coefficient,
    dbl_eps
  ) → (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
        0.75 / (max_nudging_coefficient + dbl_eps),
        (⇑(λ(__arg0) → abs(·__arg0)))(fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff)
      );
  _add_analysis_increments_to_vn = λ(vn_incr, vn, iau_wgt_dyn) → 
    (λ(vn_incr_wpᐞ0) → 
       (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)((⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                                vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(iau_wgt_dyn, vn_incr_wpᐞ0)
                              )))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(vn_incr));
  _add_temporal_tendencies_to_vn_by_interpolating_between_time_levels = λ(
    vn_nnow,
    ddt_vn_apc_ntl1,
    ddt_vn_apc_ntl2,
    ddt_vn_phy,
    z_theta_v_e,
    z_gradh_exner,
    dtime,
    wgt_nnow_vel,
    wgt_nnew_vel,
    cpd
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(ddt_vn_phy_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(z_gradh_exner_wpᐞ0) → 
             (λ(ddt_vn_apc_ntl1_wpᐞ0) → 
                (λ(ddt_vn_apc_ntl2_wpᐞ0) → 
                   (λ(vn_nnew_wpᐞ0) → vn_nnew_wpᐞ0)(
                     (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                       vn_nnow,
                       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                         dtime, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                                    (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                                      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(wgt_nnow_vel, ddt_vn_apc_ntl1_wpᐞ0),
                                      (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(wgt_nnew_vel, ddt_vn_apc_ntl2_wpᐞ0)
                                    ), ddt_vn_phy_wpᐞ0
                                  ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                                       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(cpd, z_theta_v_e), z_gradh_exner_wpᐞ0
                                     )
                                )
                       )
                     )
                   ))(__tuple_tmp_0[3]))(__tuple_tmp_0[2]))(__tuple_tmp_0[1]))(__tuple_tmp_0[0]))(
      (λ(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f) → 
         {(⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f[0]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f[1]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f[2]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f[3])})(
        {ddt_vn_phy, z_gradh_exner, ddt_vn_apc_ntl1, ddt_vn_apc_ntl2}
      )
    );
  _add_vertical_wind_derivative_to_divergence_damping = λ(
    hmask_dd3d,
    scalfac_dd3d,
    inv_dual_edge_length,
    z_dwdz_dd,
    z_graddiv_vn
  ) → 
    (λ(z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ0) → 
       (λ(scalfac_dd3dᐞ0) → 
          (λ(z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ1) → (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ1))(
            (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
              z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ0,
              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                  (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(hmask_dd3d, scalfac_dd3dᐞ0), inv_dual_edge_length
                ), (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(
                     (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                       (⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2Cₒ, 1ₒ⟫(__it)))(z_dwdz_dd), (⇑(λ(__it) → ·⟪E2Cₒ, 0ₒ⟫(__it)))(z_dwdz_dd)
                     )
                   )
              )
            )
          ))(broadcast(scalfac_dd3d, {Edgeₕ, Kᵥ})))(
      (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_graddiv_vn)
    );
  _apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping = λ(z_graddiv_vn, vn, scal_divdamp_o2) → 
    (λ(z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ0) → 
       (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(
         (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
           vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(scal_divdamp_o2, z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ0)
         )
       ))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_graddiv_vn));
  _apply_4th_order_divergence_damping = λ(
    interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
    z_graddiv2_vn,
    vn,
    divdamp_order,
    mean_cell_area,
    second_order_divdamp_factor
  ) → 
    (λ(scal_divdampᐞ0) → 
       (λ(z_graddiv2_vn_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(
            (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
              vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(scal_divdampᐞ0, z_graddiv2_vn_wpᐞ0)
            )
          ))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_graddiv2_vn)))(
      _calculate_fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff(
        interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor, divdamp_order, mean_cell_area, second_order_divdamp_factor
      )
    );
  _apply_weighted_2nd_and_4th_order_divergence_damping = λ(
    interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
    nudgecoeff_e,
    z_graddiv2_vn,
    vn,
    divdamp_order,
    mean_cell_area,
    second_order_divdamp_factor,
    max_nudging_coefficient,
    dbl_eps
  ) → 
    (λ(scal_divdampᐞ0) → 
       (λ(bdy_divdampᐞ0) → 
          (λ(z_graddiv2_vn_wpᐞ0) → 
             (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)(
               (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                 vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                         scal_divdampᐞ0, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(bdy_divdampᐞ0, nudgecoeff_e)
                       ), z_graddiv2_vn_wpᐞ0
                     )
               )
             ))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_graddiv2_vn)))(
         _calculate_reduced_fourth_order_divdamp_coeff_at_nest_boundary(
           scal_divdampᐞ0, max_nudging_coefficient, dbl_eps
         )
       ))(_calculate_fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff(
            interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor, divdamp_order, mean_cell_area, second_order_divdamp_factor
          ));
  _compute_graddiv2_of_vn = λ(geofac_grdiv, z_graddiv_vn) → 
    (λ(z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ0) → 
       (λ(z_graddiv2_vn_wpᐞ0) → (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_graddiv2_vn_wpᐞ0))(
         (⇑(λ(__arg0) → reduce(plus, 0)(·__arg0)))(
           (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → map_(multiplies)(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(
             (⇑(λ(it) → neighbors(E2C2EOₒ, it)))(z_graddiv_vn_wpᐞ0), geofac_grdiv
           )
         )
       ))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(z_graddiv_vn));
  _apply_divergence_damping_and_update_vn = λ(
    horizontal_gradient_of_normal_wind_divergence,
    next_vn,
    current_vn,
    dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels,
    predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
    corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
    normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process,
    normal_wind_iau_increment,
    second_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
    theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels,
    horizontal_pressure_gradient,
    horizontal_mask_for_3d_divdamp,
    scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,
    inv_dual_edge_length,
    nudgecoeff_e,
    geofac_grdiv,
    interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
    advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
    advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
    dtime,
    iau_wgt_dyn,
    is_iau_active,
    limited_area,
    apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping,
    apply_4th_order_divergence_damping,
    divdamp_order,
    mean_cell_area,
    second_order_divdamp_factor,
    max_nudging_coefficient,
    dbl_eps
  ) → 
    (λ(horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0) → 
       (λ(next_vnᐞ0) → 
          (λ(__if_stmt_result) → 
             (λ(next_vnᐞ1) → 
                (λ(__if_stmt_result) → 
                   (λ(next_vnᐞ2) → 
                      (λ(__if_stmt_result) → (λ(next_vnᐞ3) → next_vnᐞ3)(__if_stmt_result[0]))(
                        if   is_iau_active
                        then (λ(next_vnᐞ3) → {next_vnᐞ3})(
                               _add_analysis_increments_to_vn(normal_wind_iau_increment, next_vnᐞ2, iau_wgt_dyn)
                             )
                        else (λ(next_vnᐞ3) → {next_vnᐞ3})(next_vnᐞ2)
                      ))(__if_stmt_result[0]))(
                  if   apply_4th_order_divergence_damping
                  then (λ(squared_horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0) → 
                          (λ(__if_stmt_result) → (λ(next_vnᐞ2) → {next_vnᐞ2})(__if_stmt_result[0]))(
                            if   limited_area
                            then (λ(next_vnᐞ2) → {next_vnᐞ2})(_apply_weighted_2nd_and_4th_order_divergence_damping(
                                                                interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
                                                                nudgecoeff_e,
                                                                squared_horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0,
                                                                next_vnᐞ1,
                                                                divdamp_order,
                                                                mean_cell_area,
                                                                second_order_divdamp_factor,
                                                                max_nudging_coefficient,
                                                                dbl_eps
                                                              ))
                            else (λ(next_vnᐞ2) → {next_vnᐞ2})(
                                   _apply_4th_order_divergence_damping(interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
                                                                       squared_horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0,
                                                                       next_vnᐞ1,
                                                                       divdamp_order,
                                                                       mean_cell_area,
                                                                       second_order_divdamp_factor)
                                 )
                          ))(_compute_graddiv2_of_vn(geofac_grdiv, horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0))
                  else (λ(next_vnᐞ2) → {next_vnᐞ2})(next_vnᐞ1)
                ))(__if_stmt_result[0]))(
            if   apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping
            then (λ(next_vnᐞ1) → {next_vnᐞ1})(
                   _apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping(
                     horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0, next_vnᐞ0, second_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff
                   )
                 )
            else (λ(next_vnᐞ1) → {next_vnᐞ1})(next_vnᐞ0)
          ))(_add_temporal_tendencies_to_vn_by_interpolating_between_time_levels(
               current_vn,
               predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
               corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
               normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process,
               theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels,
               horizontal_pressure_gradient,
               dtime,
               advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
               advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
               1004.64
             )))(_add_vertical_wind_derivative_to_divergence_damping(horizontal_mask_for_3d_divdamp,
                                                                     scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,
                                                                     inv_dual_edge_length,
                                                                     dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels,
                                                                     horizontal_gradient_of_normal_wind_divergence));
  next_vn @ u⟨ Edgeₕ: [horizontal_start, horizontal_end[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
       ← _apply_divergence_damping_and_update_vn(horizontal_gradient_of_normal_wind_divergence,
                                                 next_vn,
                                                 current_vn,
                                                 dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels,
                                                 predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
                                                 corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
                                                 normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process,
                                                 normal_wind_iau_increment,
                                                 second_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
                                                 theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels,
                                                 horizontal_pressure_gradient,
                                                 horizontal_mask_for_3d_divdamp,
                                                 scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,
                                                 inv_dual_edge_length,
                                                 nudgecoeff_e,
                                                 geofac_grdiv,
                                                 interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
                                                 advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
                                                 advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
                                                 dtime,
                                                 iau_wgt_dyn,
                                                 is_iau_active,
                                                 limited_area,
                                                 apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping,
                                                 apply_4th_order_divergence_damping,
                                                 divdamp_order,
                                                 mean_cell_area,
                                                 second_order_divdamp_factor,
                                                 max_nudging_coefficient,
                                                 dbl_eps);
}
============================================================


============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER INLINING FUNDEFS ===
============================================================
apply_divergence_damping_and_update_vn(
  horizontal_gradient_of_normal_wind_divergence,
  next_vn,
  current_vn,
  dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels,
  predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process,
  normal_wind_iau_increment,
  second_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
  theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels,
  horizontal_pressure_gradient,
  horizontal_mask_for_3d_divdamp,
  scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,
  inv_dual_edge_length,
  nudgecoeff_e,
  geofac_grdiv,
  interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
  advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
  advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
  dtime,
  iau_wgt_dyn,
  is_iau_active,
  limited_area,
  apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping,
  apply_4th_order_divergence_damping,
  divdamp_order,
  mean_cell_area,
  second_order_divdamp_factor,
  max_nudging_coefficient,
  dbl_eps,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  _calculate_fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff = λ(
    interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
    divdamp_order,
    mean_cell_area,
    second_order_divdamp_factor
  ) → 
    (λ(interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factorᐞ0) → 
       (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
         (⇑(λ(__arg0) → neg(·__arg0)))(interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factorᐞ0), power(mean_cell_area, 2)
       ))(if   divdamp_order == 24
          then (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → maximum(·__arg0, ·__arg1)))(
                 0.0, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                        interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor, 0.25 × second_order_divdamp_factor
                      )
               )
          else interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor);
  _calculate_reduced_fourth_order_divdamp_coeff_at_nest_boundary = λ(
    fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
    max_nudging_coefficient,
    dbl_eps
  ) → (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
        0.75 / (max_nudging_coefficient + dbl_eps),
        (⇑(λ(__arg0) → abs(·__arg0)))(fourth_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff)
      );
  _add_analysis_increments_to_vn = λ(vn_incr, vn, iau_wgt_dyn) → 
    (λ(vn_incr_wpᐞ0) → 
       (λ(vn_wpᐞ0) → vn_wpᐞ0)((⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                                vn, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(iau_wgt_dyn, vn_incr_wpᐞ0)
                              )))((⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(vn_incr));
  _add_temporal_tendencies_to_vn_by_interpolating_between_time_levels = λ(
    vn_nnow,
    ddt_vn_apc_ntl1,
    ddt_vn_apc_ntl2,
    ddt_vn_phy,
    z_theta_v_e,
    z_gradh_exner,
    dtime,
    wgt_nnow_vel,
    wgt_nnew_vel,
    cpd
  ) → 
    (λ(__tuple_tmp_0) → 
       (λ(ddt_vn_phy_wpᐞ0, z_gradh_exner_wpᐞ0, ddt_vn_apc_ntl1_wpᐞ0, ddt_vn_apc_ntl2_wpᐞ0) → 
          (λ(vn_nnew_wpᐞ0) → vn_nnew_wpᐞ0)(
            (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
              vn_nnow,
              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                dtime, (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 - ·__arg1))(
                         (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                           (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 + ·__arg1))(
                             (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(wgt_nnow_vel, ddt_vn_apc_ntl1_wpᐞ0),
                             (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(wgt_nnew_vel, ddt_vn_apc_ntl2_wpᐞ0)
                           ), ddt_vn_phy_wpᐞ0
                         ), (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(
                              (⇑(λ(__arg0, __arg1) → ·__arg0 × ·__arg1))(cpd, z_theta_v_e), z_gradh_exner_wpᐞ0
                            )
                       )
              )
            )
          ))(__tuple_tmp_0[0], __tuple_tmp_0[1], __tuple_tmp_0[2], __tuple_tmp_0[3]))(
      (λ(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f) → 
         {(⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f[0]),
          (⇑(λ(__arg0) → (λ(val) → cast_(val, float64))(·__arg0)))(__val_dde4e9fb02f2cd6f[1]),

=== GTIR AFTER CARTESIAN UNROLLING ===

apply_divergence_damping_and_update_vn(
  horizontal_gradient_of_normal_wind_divergence,
  next_vn,
  current_vn,
  dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels,
  predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process,
  normal_wind_iau_increment,
  second_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
  theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels,
  horizontal_pressure_gradient,
  horizontal_mask_for_3d_divdamp,
  scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,
  inv_dual_edge_length,
  nudgecoeff_e,
  geofac_grdiv,
  interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
  advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
  advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
  dtime,
  iau_wgt_dyn,
  is_iau_active,
  limited_area,
  apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping,
  apply_4th_order_divergence_damping,
  divdamp_order,
  mean_cell_area,
  second_order_divdamp_factor,
  max_nudging_coefficient,
  dbl_eps,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  next_vn @ c⟨ IDimₕ: [4ₒ, 13ₒ[, JDimₕ: [4ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 3ₒ[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
       ← concat_where(
           c⟨ Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 1ₒ[ ⟩ ∧ (c⟨ IDimₕ: [5ₒ, 12ₒ[ ⟩ ∧ c⟨ JDimₕ: [4ₒ, 9ₒ[ ⟩),
           (λ(horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0, next_vnᐞ0) → 
              (λ(next_vnᐞ1) → 
                 (λ(next_vnᐞ2) → 
                    if   is_iau_active
                    then (⇑(λ(__arg0____, __arg1___) → ·__arg0____ + ·__arg1___))(
                           next_vnᐞ2, (⇑(λ(__arg0____, __arg1___) → ·__arg0____ × ·__arg1___))(
                                        iau_wgt_dyn, (⇑(λ(__arg0___) → cast_(·__arg0___, float64)))(normal_wind_iau_increment)
                                      )
                         )
                    else next_vnᐞ2)(
                   if   apply_4th_order_divergence_damping
                   then (λ(squared_horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0) → 
                           if   limited_area
                           then (λ(scal_divdampᐞ0) → 
                                   (⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ + ·__arg1_))(
                                     next_vnᐞ1,
                                     (⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ × ·__arg1_))(
                                       (⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ + ·__arg1_))(
                                         scal_divdampᐞ0,

============================================================
=== GTIR AFTER CANONICALIZING DOMAIN ARGUMENTS ===
============================================================
apply_divergence_damping_and_update_vn(
  horizontal_gradient_of_normal_wind_divergence,
  next_vn,
  current_vn,
  dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels,
  predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency,
  normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process,
  normal_wind_iau_increment,
  second_order_divdamp_scaling_coeff,
  theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels,
  horizontal_pressure_gradient,
  horizontal_mask_for_3d_divdamp,
  scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,
  inv_dual_edge_length,
  nudgecoeff_e,
  geofac_grdiv,
  interpolated_fourth_order_divdamp_factor,
  advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
  advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
  dtime,
  iau_wgt_dyn,
  is_iau_active,
  limited_area,
  apply_2nd_order_divergence_damping,
  apply_4th_order_divergence_damping,
  divdamp_order,
  mean_cell_area,
  second_order_divdamp_factor,
  max_nudging_coefficient,
  dbl_eps,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  next_vn @ c⟨ IDimₕ: [4ₒ, 13ₒ[, JDimₕ: [4ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 3ₒ[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
       ← (λ(__cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b) → 
            (λ(__cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b) → 
               concat_where(c⟨ Kolorₕ: [-∞, 0ₒ[ ⟩,
                            __cwcda_field_a,
                            concat_where(c⟨ Kolorₕ: [1ₒ, ∞[ ⟩, __cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b)))(
              __cwcda_field_b,
              (λ(__cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b) → 
                 (λ(__cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b) → 
                    concat_where(c⟨ IDimₕ: [-∞, 5ₒ[ ⟩,
                                 __cwcda_field_a,
                                 concat_where(c⟨ IDimₕ: [12ₒ, ∞[ ⟩, __cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b)))(
                   __cwcda_field_b,
                   (λ(__cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b) → 
                      concat_where(c⟨ JDimₕ: [-∞, 4ₒ[ ⟩,
                                   __cwcda_field_a,
                                   concat_where(c⟨ JDimₕ: [9ₒ, ∞[ ⟩, __cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b)))(
                     __cwcda_field_b, __cwcda_field_a
                   )
                 ))(__cwcda_field_a, __cwcda_field_b)
            ))(
           (λ(horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0, next_vnᐞ0) → 
              (λ(next_vnᐞ1) → 
                 (λ(next_vnᐞ2) → 
                    if   is_iau_active
                    then (⇑(λ(__arg0____, __arg1___) → ·__arg0____ + ·__arg1___))(
                           next_vnᐞ2, (⇑(λ(__arg0____, __arg1___) → ·__arg0____ × ·__arg1___))(
                                        iau_wgt_dyn, (⇑(λ(__arg0___) → cast_(·__arg0___, float64)))(normal_wind_iau_increment)
                                      )
                         )
                    else next_vnᐞ2)(
                   if   apply_4th_order_divergence_damping
                   then (λ(squared_horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0) → 
                           if   limited_area
                           then (λ(scal_divdampᐞ0) → 
                                   (⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ + ·__arg1_))(
                                     next_vnᐞ1,
                                     (⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ × ·__arg1_))(
                                       (⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ + ·__arg1_))(
                                         scal_divdampᐞ0,
                                         (⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ × ·__arg1_))(
                                           (⇑(λ(__arg0_____, __arg1____) → ·__arg0_____ × ·__arg1____))(
                                             0.75 / (max_nudging_coefficient + dbl_eps), (⇑(λ(__arg0_____) → abs(·__arg0_____)))(scal_divdampᐞ0)
                                           ), nudgecoeff_e
                                         )
                                       ), (⇑(λ(__arg0_____) → cast_(·__arg0_____, float64)))(
                                            squared_horizontal_gradient_of_total_divergenceᐞ0
                                          )
                                     )
                                   ))((⇑(λ(__arg0______, __arg1_) → ·__arg0______ × ·__arg1_))(
                                        (⇑(λ(__arg0______) → neg(·__arg0______)))(

                                                         c⟨ IDimₕ: [4ₒ, 13ₒ[, JDimₕ: [4ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 3ₒ[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
                                                       )(dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels),
                                                       as_fieldop(
                                                         λ(__cart_unroll_it) → ·⟪_OffJDimₒ, -1ₒ⟫(__cart_unroll_it),
                                                         c⟨ IDimₕ: [4ₒ, 13ₒ[, JDimₕ: [4ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 3ₒ[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
                                                       )(dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels)
                                                     ), as_fieldop(
                                                          λ(__cart_unroll_it) → ·__cart_unroll_it,
                                                          c⟨ IDimₕ: [4ₒ, 13ₒ[, JDimₕ: [4ₒ, 10ₒ[, Kolorₕ: [0ₒ, 3ₒ[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩
                                                        )(dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels)
                                                   ),

                                                      concat_where(c⟨ Kolorₕ: [-∞, 0ₒ[ ⟩,
                                                                   __cwcda_field_a,

                                                       )(dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels),

                                                       )(dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels)
                                                     ), as_fieldop(

                                                        )(dwdz_at_cells_on_model_levels)
                                                   )
                                                 )
                                               )
                                             )
                                           )
                                         ),
                                         (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ + ·__arg1))(
                                           current_vn,
                                           (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ × ·__arg1))(
                                             dtime,
                                             (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ - ·__arg1))(
                                               (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ + ·__arg1))(
                                                 (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ + ·__arg1))(
                                                   (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ × ·__arg1))(
                                                     advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
                                                     (⇑(λ(__arg0) → cast_(·__arg0, float64)))(predictor_normal_wind_advective_tendency)
                                                   ), (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ × ·__arg1))(
                                                        advection_implicit_weight_parameter,
                                                        (⇑(λ(__arg0) → cast_(·__arg0, float64)))(corrector_normal_wind_advective_tendency)
                                                      )
                                                 ), (⇑(λ(__arg0) → cast_(·__arg0, float64)))(normal_wind_tendency_due_to_slow_physics_process)
                                               ),
                                               (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ × ·__arg1))(
                                                 (⇑(λ(__arg0_______, __arg1) → ·__arg0_______ × ·__arg1))(1004.64, theta_v_at_edges_on_model_levels),
                                                 (⇑(λ(__arg0) → cast_(·__arg0, float64)))(horizontal_pressure_gradient)
                                               )
                                             )
                                           )
                                         )), next_vn
             )
           )
         );
}

  scaling_factor_for_3d_divdamp,

  advection_explicit_weight_parameter,
  advection_implicit_weight_parameter,

  apply_4th_order_divergence_damping,

  mean_cell_area,
  second_order_divdamp_factor,
  max_nudging_coefficient,
  dbl_eps,
  horizontal_start,
  horizontal_end,
  vertical_start,
  vertical_end
) {
  __tmp_0 = temporary(
    domain=c⟨ IDimₕ: [3, 13[, JDimₕ: [3, 10[, Kolorₕ: [0, 3[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩,
    dtype=float64
  );

    domain=c⟨ IDimₕ: [4, 12[, JDimₕ: [4, 9[, Kolorₕ: [2, 3[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩,
    dtype=float64
  );
  __tmp_2 = temporary(
    domain=c⟨ IDimₕ: [3, 13[, JDimₕ: [4, 10[, Kolorₕ: [-1, 3[, Kᵥ: [vertical_start, vertical_end[ ⟩,
    dtype=float64
